// snapshotted at their current values so the copy is independent of the VM stack
// where the closure was created. The function bytecode (instructions, constants
// and imports) is preserved from the function's original context when one is
// available; only globals and the allocation limit are rebound to ctx.
func (o *CompiledFunction) bindContext(ctx *functionContext) *CompiledFunction {
	bytecode := ctx.bytecode
	if o.ctx != nil {
		bytecode = o.ctx.bytecode
	}
	free := make([]*ObjectPtr, len(o.Free))
	for i, p := range o.Free {
		if p != nil {
			val := *p.Value
			free[i] = &ObjectPtr{Value: &val}
		}

		NumLocals:     o.NumLocals,

		SourceMap:     o.SourceMap,
		Free:          free,
		ctx: &functionContext{
			bytecode:  bytecode,
			globals:   ctx.globals,
			maxAllocs: ctx.maxAllocs,
		},
	}
}

	return false

	return true

# Refactor Run error formatting into formatError and add runFunction
old='''// Run starts the execution.
func (v *VM) Run() (err error) {
	// reset VM states
	v.sp = 0
	v.curFrame = &(v.frames[0])
	v.curInsts = v.curFrame.fn.Instructions
	v.framesIndex = 1
	v.ip = -1
	v.allocs = v.maxAllocs + 1

	v.run()
	atomic.StoreInt64(&v.aborting, 0)
	err = v.err
	if err != nil {
		filePos := v.fileSet.Position(
			v.curFrame.fn.SourcePos(v.ip - 1))
		err = fmt.Errorf("Runtime Error: %w\\n\\tat %s",
			err, filePos)
		for v.framesIndex > 1 {
			v.framesIndex--
			v.curFrame = &v.frames[v.framesIndex-1]
			filePos = v.fileSet.Position(
				v.curFrame.fn.SourcePos(v.curFrame.ip - 1))
			err = fmt.Errorf("%w\\n\\tat %s", err, filePos)
		}
		return err
	}
	return nil
}'''
new='''// Run starts the execution.
func (v *VM) Run() (err error) {
	// reset VM states
	v.sp = 0
	v.curFrame = &(v.frames[0])
	v.curInsts = v.curFrame.fn.Instructions
	v.framesIndex = 1
	v.ip = -1
	v.allocs = v.maxAllocs + 1

	v.run()
	atomic.StoreInt64(&v.aborting, 0)
	err = v.err
	if err != nil {
		return v.formatError(err)
	}
	return nil
}

// runFunction runs a single compiled function with the given arguments and
// returns its return value. It is used to support invoking compiled functions
// from Go code (CompiledFunction.Call).
func (v *VM) runFunction(fn *CompiledFunction, args []Object) (
	Object, error) {
	v.stopOnReturn = true
	v.returnValue = UndefinedValue
	v.frames[0].fn = fn
	v.frames[0].freeVars = fn.Free
	v.frames[0].ip = -1
	v.frames[0].basePointer = 0
	v.curFrame = &v.frames[0]
	v.curInsts = fn.Instructions
	v.framesIndex = 1
	v.ip = -1
	v.sp = 0
	for i, arg := range args {
		v.stack[i] = arg
	}
	v.sp = fn.NumLocals
	v.allocs = v.maxAllocs + 1

	v.run()
	atomic.StoreInt64(&v.aborting, 0)
	if v.err != nil {
		return nil, v.formatError(v.err)
	}
	ret := v.returnValue
	if ret == nil {
		ret = UndefinedValue
	}
	return ret, nil
}

// formatError wraps a runtime error with the runtime error message and the
// source position / call stack.
func (v *VM) formatError(err error) error {
	filePos := v.fileSet.Position(
		v.curFrame.fn.SourcePos(v.ip - 1))
	err = fmt.Errorf("Runtime Error: %w\\n\\tat %s",
		err, filePos)
	for v.framesIndex > 1 {
		v.framesIndex--
		v.curFrame = &v.frames[v.framesIndex-1]
		filePos = v.fileSet.Position(
			v.curFrame.fn.SourcePos(v.curFrame.ip - 1))
		err = fmt.Errorf("%w\\n\\tat %s", err, filePos)
	}
	return err
}'''

}

// RunContext is like Run but includes a context.
func (s *Script) RunContext(
	ctx context.Context,
) (compiled *Compiled, err error) {
	compiled, err = s.Compile()
	if err != nil {
		return
	}
	err = compiled.RunContext(ctx)
	return
}

func (s *Script) prepCompile() (
	symbolTable *SymbolTable,
	globals []Object,
	err error,
) {
	var names []string
	for name := range s.variables {
		names = append(names, name)
	}

	symbolTable = NewSymbolTable()
	for idx, fn := range builtinFuncs {
		symbolTable.DefineBuiltin(idx, fn.Name)
	}

	globals = make([]Object, GlobalsSize)

	for idx, name := range names {
		symbol := symbolTable.Define(name)
		if symbol.Index != idx {
			panic(fmt.Errorf("wrong symbol index: %d != %d",
				idx, symbol.Index))
		}
		globals[symbol.Index] = s.variables[name].value
	}
	return
}

// Compiled is a compiled instance of the user script. Use Script.Compile() to
// create Compiled object.
type Compiled struct {
	globalIndexes map[string]int // global symbol name to index
	bytecode      *Bytecode
	globals       []Object
	maxAllocs     int64
	lock          sync.RWMutex
}

// Run executes the compiled script in the virtual machine.
func (c *Compiled) Run() error {
	c.lock.Lock()
	defer c.lock.Unlock()

	v := NewVM(c.bytecode, c.globals, c.maxAllocs)
	return v.Run()
}

// RunContext is like Run but includes a context.
func (c *Compiled) RunContext(ctx context.Context) (err error) {
	c.lock.Lock()
	defer c.lock.Unlock()

	v := NewVM(c.bytecode, c.globals, c.maxAllocs)
	ch := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() {
		defer func() {
			if r := recover(); r != nil {
				switch e := r.(type) {
				case string:
					ch <- fmt.Errorf(e)
				case error:
					ch <- e
				default:
					ch <- fmt.Errorf("unknown panic: %v", e)
				}
			}
		}()
		ch <- v.Run()
	}()

	select {
	case <-ctx.Done():
		v.Abort()
		<-ch
		err = ctx.Err()
	case err = <-ch:
	}
	return
}

// Size of compiled script in bytes
// (as much as we can calculate it without reflection and black magic)
func (c *Compiled) Size() int64 {
	c.lock.RLock()
	defer c.lock.RUnlock()

	return c.bytecode.Size() + int64(len(c.globalIndexes)+len(c.globals))
}

// Clone creates a new copy of Compiled. Cloned copies are safe for concurrent
// use by multiple goroutines.
func (c *Compiled) Clone() *Compiled {
	c.lock.RLock()
	defer c.lock.RUnlock()

	clone := &Compiled{
		globalIndexes: c.globalIndexes,
		bytecode:      c.bytecode,
		globals:       make([]Object, len(c.globals)),
		maxAllocs:     c.maxAllocs,
	}
	// copy global objects
	for idx, g := range c.globals {
		if g != nil {
			clone.globals[idx] = g.Copy()
		}
	}
	return clone
}

// IsDefined returns true if the variable name is defined (has value) before or
// after the execution.
func (c *Compiled) IsDefined(name string) bool {
	c.lock.RLock()
	defer c.lock.RUnlock()

	idx, ok := c.globalIndexes[name]
	if !ok {
		return false
	}
	v := c.globals[idx]
	if v == nil {
		return false
	}
	return v != UndefinedValue
}

// Get returns a variable identified by the name.
func (c *Compiled) Get(name string) *Variable {
	c.lock.RLock()
	defer c.lock.RUnlock()

	value := UndefinedValue
	if idx, ok := c.globalIndexes[name]; ok {
		value = c.globals[idx]
		if value == nil {
			value = UndefinedValue
		}
	}
	return &Variable{
		name:  name,
		value: value,
	}
}

returncode=0
// Compile compiles the script with all the defined variables, and, returns
// Compiled object.
func (s *Script) Compile() (*Compiled, error) {
	symbolTable, globals, err := s.prepCompile()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	fileSet := parser.NewFileSet()
	srcFile := fileSet.AddFile("(main)", -1, len(s.input))
	p := parser.NewParser(srcFile, s.input, nil)
	file, err := p.ParseFile()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	c := NewCompiler(srcFile, symbolTable, nil, s.modules, nil)
	c.EnableFileImport(s.enableFileImport)
	c.SetImportDir(s.importDir)
	if err := c.Compile(file); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	// reduce globals size
	globals = globals[:symbolTable.MaxSymbols()+1]

	// global symbol names to indexes
	globalIndexes := make(map[string]int, len(globals))
	for _, name := range symbolTable.Names() {
		symbol, _, _ := symbolTable.Resolve(name, false)
		if symbol.Scope == ScopeGlobal {
			globalIndexes[name] = symbol.Index
		}
	}

	// remove duplicates from constants
	bytecode := c.Bytecode()
	bytecode.RemoveDuplicates()

	// check the constant objects limit
	if s.maxConstObjects >= 0 {
		cnt := bytecode.CountObjects()
		if cnt > s.maxConstObjects {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("exceeding constant objects limit: %d", cnt)
		}
	}
	return &Compiled{
		globalIndexes: globalIndexes,
		bytecode:      bytecode,
		globals:       globals,
		maxAllocs:     s.maxAllocs,
	}, nil
}

// Run compiles and runs the scripts. Use returned compiled object to access
// global variables.

END STEP

p='script.go'

		globalIndexes: globalIndexes,
		bytecode:      bytecode,
		globals:       globals,
		maxAllocs:     s.maxAllocs,

new='''	compiled := &Compiled{
		globalIndexes: globalIndexes,
		bytecode:      bytecode,
		globals:       globals,
		maxAllocs:     s.maxAllocs,
	}
	compiled.ctx = &functionContext{
		bytecode:  bytecode,
		globals:   globals,
		maxAllocs: s.maxAllocs,
	}

	// bind any callable values provided by the user to this compiled
	// instance so they execute against this instance's globals.
	for idx, g := range globals {
		if g != nil {
			globals[idx] = bindObject(g, compiled.ctx)
		}
	}
	return compiled, nil
}'''

	globalIndexes map[string]int // global symbol name to index
	bytecode      *Bytecode
	globals       []Object
	maxAllocs     int64

}'''
new='''type Compiled struct {
	globalIndexes map[string]int // global symbol name to index
	bytecode      *Bytecode
	globals       []Object
	maxAllocs     int64
	ctx           *functionContext
	lock          sync.RWMutex
}'''

		globalIndexes: c.globalIndexes,
		bytecode:      c.bytecode,

		maxAllocs:     c.maxAllocs,

		}

		globalIndexes: c.globalIndexes,
		bytecode:      c.bytecode,
		globals:       make([]Object, len(c.globals)),
		maxAllocs:     c.maxAllocs,
	}
	clone.ctx = &functionContext{
		bytecode:  c.bytecode,
		globals:   clone.globals,
		maxAllocs: c.maxAllocs,
	}
	// copy global objects and rebind any callable values to the clone's
	// execution context. Captured free variables are snapshotted at clone
	// time so the clone is fully isolated from the source instance.
	for idx, g := range c.globals {
		if g != nil {
			clone.globals[idx] = bindObject(g, clone.ctx)
		}
	}
	return clone
}'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)

old='''	idx, ok := c.globalIndexes[name]
	if !ok {
		return fmt.Errorf("'%s' is not defined", name)
	}
	c.globals[idx] = obj
	return nil
}'''
new='''	idx, ok := c.globalIndexes[name]
	if !ok {
		return fmt.Errorf("'%s' is not defined", name)
	}
	// bind the value (and any callable values reachable inside arrays/maps)
	// to this compiled instance. Callables are rebound to this instance's
	// globals while their captured free variables are snapshotted, isolating
	// this instance from the value's source instance.
	c.globals[idx] = bindObject(obj.Copy(), c.ctx)
	return nil
}'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)

package tengo_test

import (
	"fmt"
	"testing"

	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2"
)

func runScript(t *testing.T, src string) *tengo.Compiled {
	t.Helper()
	c, err := tengo.NewScript([]byte(src)).Run()
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("run: %v", err) }
	return c
}

func TestGoCallBasic(t *testing.T) {
	c := runScript(t, `
add := func(a, b) { return a + b }
out := add(1, 2)
`)
	fn, _ := c.Get("add").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	ret, err := fn.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 40}, &tengo.Int{Value: 2})
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if ret.String() != "42" { t.Fatalf("got %s", ret) }
}

func TestGoCallRecursion(t *testing.T) {
	c := runScript(t, `
fact := func(n) {
	if n <= 1 { return 1 }
	return n * fact(n-1)
}
`)
	fn, _ := c.Get("fact").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	ret, err := fn.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 10})
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if ret.String() != "3628800" { t.Fatalf("got %s", ret) }
}

func TestGoCallVariadic(t *testing.T) {
	c := runScript(t, `
sum := func(a, args...) {
	s := a
	for _, x in args { s += x }
	return s
}
`)
	fn, _ := c.Get("sum").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	ret, err := fn.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 1}, &tengo.Int{Value: 2}, &tengo.Int{Value: 3})
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if ret.String() != "6" { t.Fatalf("got %s", ret) }
}

func TestGoCallReturnedClosure(t *testing.T) {
	c := runScript(t, `
make := func(x) {
	return func(y) { return x + y }
}
f := make(10)
`)
	makeFn, _ := c.Get("make").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	ret, err := makeFn.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 10})
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	inner, ok := ret.(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	if !ok { t.Fatalf("ret type %T", ret) }
	ret2, err := inner.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 32})
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if ret2.String() != "42" { t.Fatalf("got %s", ret2) }
}

func TestGoCallErrorFormat(t *testing.T) {
	c := runScript(t, `
boom := func() { return 1 + "x" }
`)
	fn, _ := c.Get("boom").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	_, err := fn.Call()
	if err == nil { t.Fatal("expected error") }
	fmt.Println("ERR:", err)
}

func TestGoCallCallbackArg(t *testing.T) {
	// Go builtin calls a script function passed as argument
	caller := func(args ...tengo.Object) (tengo.Object, error) {
		fn := args[0].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
		return fn.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 20})
	}
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(`
caller := func(fn) { return fn(5) }
gcall := import("gcall")
out := gcall.f(caller)
`))
	mods := tengo.NewModuleMap()
	mods.AddBuiltinModule("gcall", map[string]tengo.Object{
		"f": &tengo.UserFunction{Name: "f", Value: caller},
	})
	s.SetImports(mods)
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	// Actually test that script passes a function to Go which calls it.
	_ = c
}

func TestGoCallTransferIsolation(t *testing.T) {
	src1 := `
g := 10
counter := func() {
	n := 0
	return func() { n++; return g + n }
}
f := counter()
f()
f()
`
	s2src := `
out := f()
`
	c1 := runScript(t, src1)
	fn, _ := c1.Get("f").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)

	// transfer to c2
	s2 := tengo.NewScript([]byte(s2src))
	if err := s2.Add("f", fn); err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	c2, err := s2.Run()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }

	// c1's f still has n=2, g=10 -> 12
	ret1, err := fn.Call()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if ret1.String() != "12" { t.Fatalf("c1 got %s", ret1) }

	// c2's f should snapshot n=2 but resolve g against c2.
	// But c2 script assigns f() to out before we can call it.
	_ = c2
	// Let's instead directly call c2.Get("f")
	// Actually c2's script references f, so f must be defined in c2's globals.
	_ = c2
}

func TestGoCallCloneIsolation(t *testing.T) {
	c1 := runScript(t, `
g := 5
mk := func() {
	n := 0
	return func() { n++; return g + n }
}
f := mk()
f()
`)
	c2 := c1.Clone()
	f1, _ := c1.Get("f").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	f2, _ := c2.Get("f").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	// mutate clone global g
	if err := c2.Set("g", 100); err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	// f2 should use clone's g=100, n snapshot=1 -> 101
	ret2, err := f2.Call()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if ret2.String() != "101" { t.Fatalf("clone got %s", ret2) }
	// f1 should use c1's g=5, n=1 -> 6
	ret1, err := f1.Call()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if ret1.String() != "6" { t.Fatalf("source got %s", ret1) }
}

func (p *Parser) parseIdentList() *IdentList {
	if p.trace {
		defer untracep(tracep(p, "IdentList"))
	}

	var params []*Ident
	lparen := p.expect(token.LParen)
	isVarArgs := false
	if p.token != token.RParen {
		if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
			isVarArgs = true
			p.next()
		}

		params = append(params, p.parseIdent())
		for !isVarArgs && p.token == token.Comma {
			p.next()
			if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
				isVarArgs = true
				p.next()
			}
			params = append(params, p.parseIdent())
		}
	}

	rparen := p.expect(token.RParen)
	return &IdentList{
		LParen:  lparen,
		RParen:  rparen,
		VarArgs: isVarArgs,
		List:    params,
	}
}

// NewVM creates a VM.
func NewVM(
	bytecode *Bytecode,
	globals []Object,
	maxAllocs int64,
) *VM {
	if globals == nil {
		globals = make([]Object, GlobalsSize)
	}
	v := &VM{
		constants:   bytecode.Constants,
		sp:          0,
		globals:     globals,
		fileSet:     bytecode.FileSet,
		framesIndex: 1,
		ip:          -1,
		maxAllocs:   maxAllocs,
	}
	v.ctx = &functionContext{
		bytecode:  bytecode,
		globals:   v.globals,
		maxAllocs: maxAllocs,
	}
	v.frames[0].fn = bytecode.MainFunction
	v.frames[0].ip = -1
	v.curFrame = &v.frames[0]
	v.curInsts = v.curFrame.fn.Instructions
	return v
}

// Abort aborts the execution.
func (v *VM) Abort() {
	atomic.StoreInt64(&v.aborting, 1)
}

// Run starts the execution.
func (v *VM) Run() (err error) {
	// reset VM states
	v.sp = 0
	v.curFrame = &(v.frames[0])
	v.curInsts = v.curFrame.fn.Instructions
	v.framesIndex = 1
	v.ip = -1
	v.allocs = v.maxAllocs + 1

	v.run()
	atomic.StoreInt64(&v.aborting, 0)
	err = v.err
	if err != nil {
		return v.formatError(err)
	}
	return nil
}

// runFunction runs a single compiled function with the given arguments and
// returns its return value. It is used to support invoking compiled functions

	src1 := `
g := 10
counter := func() {
	n := 0
	return func() { n++; return g + n }
}
f := counter()
f()
f()

out := f()
`

	fn, _ := c1.Get("f").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)

	// transfer to c2
	s2 := tengo.NewScript([]byte(s2src))
	if err := s2.Add("f", fn); err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	c2, err := s2.Run()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }

	// c1's f still has n=2, g=10 -> 12
	ret1, err := fn.Call()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if ret1.String() != "12" { t.Fatalf("c1 got %s", ret1) }

	// c2's f should snapshot n=2 but resolve g against c2.
	// But c2 script assigns f() to out before we can call it.

	// Let's instead directly call c2.Get("f")
	// Actually c2's script references f, so f must be defined in c2's globals.
	_ = c2
}''', '''func TestGoCallTransferIsolation(t *testing.T) {
	src1 := `
g := 10
counter := func() {
	n := 0
	return func() { n++; return g + n }
}
f := counter()
f()
f()
`
	c1 := runScript(t, src1)
	fn, _ := c1.Get("f").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)

	// transfer to c2 via Add + Compile
	s2src := `out := f()`
	s2 := tengo.NewScript([]byte(s2src))
	if err := s2.Add("f", fn); err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	c2, err := s2.Run()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }

	ret1, err := fn.Call()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if ret1.String() != "12" { t.Fatalf("c1 got %s", ret1) }

	// c2's f (snapshot n=2, resolves g against c2). c2's script
	// doesn't define g, so set it now.

new = '''func TestGoCallTransferIsolation(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
g := 10
mk := func() {
	n := 0
	return func() { n++; return g + n }
}
f := mk()
f()
f()
`
	c1 := runScript(t, src)
	fn1, _ := c1.Get("f").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)

	// c2 compiled from the same source so global indexes match
	c2 := runScript(t, src)
	if err := c2.Set("f", fn1); err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if err := c2.Set("g", 100); err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }

	// transferred closure snapshots n=2; calling it increments to 3 and
	// uses c2's g=100 -> 103
	fn2, _ := c2.Get("f").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	ret2, err := fn2.Call()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if ret2.String() != "103" { t.Fatalf("c2 got %s", ret2) }

	// source instance is unaffected: n=2 -> 3, g=10 -> 13
	ret1, err := fn1.Call()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if ret1.String() != "13" { t.Fatalf("c1 got %s", ret1) }

	// c2's globals were not mutated by c1's call and vice versa
	if got := c2.Get("g").Int(); got != 100 { t.Fatalf("c2 g=%d", got) }
	if got := c1.Get("g").Int(); got != 10 { t.Fatalf("c1 g=%d", got) }
}

'''

func TestCompiled_RunContext(t *testing.T) {
	// machine completes normally
	c := compile(t, `a := 5`, nil)
	err := c.RunContext(context.Background())
	require.NoError(t, err)
	compiledGet(t, c, "a", int64(5))

	// timeout
	c = compile(t, `for true {}`, nil)
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(),
		1*time.Millisecond)
	defer cancel()
	err = c.RunContext(ctx)
	require.Equal(t, context.DeadlineExceeded, err)
}

func TestCompiled_CustomObject(t *testing.T) {
	c := compile(t, `r := (t<130)`, M{"t": &customNumber{value: 123}})
	compiledRun(t, c)
	compiledGet(t, c, "r", true)

	c = compile(t, `r := (t>13)`, M{"t": &customNumber{value: 123}})
	compiledRun(t, c)
	compiledGet(t, c, "r", true)
}

// customNumber is a user defined object that can compare to tengo.Int
// very shitty implementation, just to test that token.Less and token.Greater in BinaryOp works
type customNumber struct {
	tengo.ObjectImpl
	value int64
}

func (n *customNumber) TypeName() string {
	return "Number"
}

func (n *customNumber) String() string {
	return strconv.FormatInt(n.value, 10)
}

func (n *customNumber) BinaryOp(op token.Token, rhs tengo.Object) (tengo.Object, error) {
	tengoInt, ok := rhs.(*tengo.Int)
	if !ok {
		return nil, tengo.ErrInvalidOperator
	}
	return n.binaryOpInt(op, tengoInt)
}

func (n *customNumber) binaryOpInt(op token.Token, rhs *tengo.Int) (tengo.Object, error) {
	i := n.value

	switch op {
	case token.Less:
		if i < rhs.Value {
			return tengo.TrueValue, nil
		}
		return tengo.FalseValue, nil
	case token.Greater:
		if i > rhs.Value {
			return tengo.TrueValue, nil
		}
		return tengo.FalseValue, nil
	case token.LessEq:
		if i <= rhs.Value {
			return tengo.TrueValue, nil
		}
		return tengo.FalseValue, nil
	case token.GreaterEq:
		if i >= rhs.Value {
			return tengo.TrueValue, nil
		}
		return tengo.FalseValue, nil
	}
	return nil, tengo.ErrInvalidOperator
}

func TestScript_ImportError(t *testing.T) {
	m := `
	exp := import("expression")
	r := exp(ctx)
`

	src := `
export func(ctx) {
	closure := func() {
		if ctx.actiontimes < 0 { // an error is thrown here because actiontimes is undefined
			return true
		}
		return false
	}

	return closure()
}`

	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(m))
	mods := tengo.NewModuleMap()
	mods.AddSourceModule("expression", []byte(src))
	s.SetImports(mods)

	err := s.Add("ctx", map[string]interface{}{
		"ctx": 12,
	})
	require.NoError(t, err)

	_, err = s.Run()
	require.True(t, strings.Contains(err.Error(), "expression:4:6"))
}

func compile(t *testing.T, input string, vars M) *tengo.Compiled {
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(input))
	for vn, vv := range vars {
		err := s.Add(vn, vv)
		require.NoError(t, err)
	}

	c, err := s.Compile()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NotNil(t, c)
	return c
}

func compileError(t *testing.T, input string, vars M) {
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(input))
	for vn, vv := range vars {
		err := s.Add(vn, vv)
		require.NoError(t, err)
	}
	_, err := s.Compile()
	require.Error(t, err)
}

func compiledRun(t *testing.T, c *tengo.Compiled) {
	err := c.Run()
	require.NoError(t, err)
}

func compiledGet(
	t *testing.T,
	c *tengo.Compiled,
	name string,
	expected interface{},
) {
	v := c.Get(name)

func compiledIsDefined(

func TestCompiled_Clone(t *testing.T) {
	script := tengo.NewScript([]byte(`
count += 1
data["b"] = 2
`))

	err := script.Add("data", map[string]interface{}{"a": 1})
	require.NoError(t, err)

	err = script.Add("count", 1000)
	require.NoError(t, err)

	compiled, err := script.Compile()
	require.NoError(t, err)

	clone := compiled.Clone()
	err = clone.RunContext(context.Background())
	require.NoError(t, err)

	require.Equal(t, 1000, compiled.Get("count").Int())
	require.Equal(t, 1, len(compiled.Get("data").Map()))

	require.Equal(t, 1001, clone.Get("count").Int())
	require.Equal(t, 2, len(clone.Get("data").Map()))
}

END STEP

# Change bindObject default from o.Copy() to o

	return nil
}'''
new='''	c.globals[idx] = bindObject(obj, c.ctx)
	return nil
}'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new,1)

# Clone: use g.Copy() with nil guard and bindObject
old='''	// copy global objects and rebind any callable values to the clone's
	// execution context. Captured free variables are snapshotted at clone
	// time so the clone is fully isolated from the source instance.
	for idx, g := range c.globals {
		if g != nil {
			clone.globals[idx] = bindObject(g, clone.ctx)
		}
	}
	return clone
}'''
new='''	// copy global objects and rebind any callable values to the clone's
	// execution context. Captured free variables are snapshotted at clone
	// time so the clone is fully isolated from the source instance.
	for idx, g := range c.globals {
		if g != nil {
			if cp := g.Copy(); cp != nil {
				clone.globals[idx] = bindObject(cp, clone.ctx)
			}
		}
	}
	return clone
}'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new,1)
open(p,'w').write(s)
print("done")

package tengo_test

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2"
)

func TestGoCallCompositeReturn(t *testing.T) {
	c := runScript(t, `
mk := func(x) {
	f1 := func(y) { return x + y }
	f2 := func(y) { return x * y }
	return [f1, f2]
}
`)
	fn, _ := c.Get("mk").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	ret, err := fn.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 10})
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	arr, ok := ret.(*tengo.Array)
	if !ok { t.Fatalf("type %T", ret) }
	if len(arr.Value) != 2 { t.Fatalf("len %d", len(arr.Value)) }
	f1 := arr.Value[0].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	f2 := arr.Value[1].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	r1, _ := f1.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 5})
	if r1.String() != "15" { t.Fatalf("f1 %s", r1) }
	r2, _ := f2.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 5})
	if r2.String() != "50" { t.Fatalf("f2 %s", r2) }
}

func TestGoCallNestedTransferIsolation(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
g := 5
mk := func(x) {
	n := 0
	inc := func() { n++; return g + n + x }
	return [inc, { "inc": inc }]
}
data := mk(100)
`
	c1 := runScript(t, src)
	src2 := `
out := 1
`
	c2 := runScript(t, src2)
	if err := c2.Set("data", c1.Get("data").Object()); err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if err := c2.Set("g", 50); err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }

	arrObj := c2.Get("data").Object()
	arr, ok := arrObj.(*tengo.Array)
	if !ok { t.Fatalf("type %T", arrObj) }
	incArr := arr.Value[0].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	r, err := incArr.Call()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if r.String() != "151" { t.Fatalf("arr inc got %s (want 50+1+100)", r) }

	m, ok := arr.Value[1].(*tengo.Map)
	if !ok { t.Fatalf("map type %T", arr.Value[1]) }
	incMap := m.Value["inc"].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	r2, err := incMap.Call()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if r2.String() != "152" { t.Fatalf("map inc got %s", r2) }
}

func TestGoCallSourceModuleExport(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
mod := import("mymod")
f := mod.add
g := 10
`
	modsrc := `
x := 1
add := func(a, b) { return a + b + x }
export { "add": add }
`
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(src))
	mods := tengo.NewModuleMap()
	mods.AddSourceModule("mymod", []byte(modsrc))
	s.SetImports(mods)
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	fn, ok := c.Get("f").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	if !ok { t.Fatalf("type %T", c.Get("f").Object()) }
	ret, err := fn.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 20}, &tengo.Int{Value: 21})
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if ret.String() != "42" { t.Fatalf("got %s", ret) }
}

func TestGoCallImportInside(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
math := import("math")
use := func(x) { return math.abs(x) }
`
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(src))
	s.SetImports(stdlibmod())
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	fn, _ := c.Get("use").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	ret, err := fn.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: -42})
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if ret.String() != "42" { t.Fatalf("got %s", ret) }
}

func TestGoCallGlobalMutation(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
g := 5
bump := func() { g += 10; return g }
`
	c := runScript(t, src)
	fn, _ := c.Get("bump").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	r1, err := fn.Call()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if r1.String() != "15" { t.Fatalf("r1 %s", r1) }
	r2, _ := fn.Call()
	if r2.String() != "25" { t.Fatalf("r2 %s", r2) }
	if got := c.Get("g").Int(); got != 15 { t.Fatalf("g %d", got) }
}

func TestGoCallWrongArgs(t *testing.T) {
	c := runScript(t, `add := func(a, b) { return a + b }`)
	fn, _ := c.Get("add").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	_, err := fn.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 1})
	if err == nil { t.Fatal("expected error") }
	c2 := runScript(t, `v := func(a, ...rest) { return len(rest) }`)
	fn2, _ := c2.Get("v").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	_, err = fn2.Call()
	if err == nil { t.Fatal("expected error for missing required arg") }
}

func TestGoCallClosureSharingWithinInstance(t *testing.T) {
	// two closures capturing the same local must stay shared in-script
	src := `
mk := func() {
	n := 0
	inc := func() { n++; return n }
	get := func() { return n }
	return [inc, get]
}
pair := mk()
inc := pair[0]
get := pair[1]
inc()
inc()
out := get()
`
	c := runScript(t, src)
	if got := c.Get("out").Int(); got != 2 { t.Fatalf("out %d", got) }
	incFn, _ := c.Get("inc").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	getFn, _ := c.Get("get").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	// Go-side call: inc increments shared n to 3, get sees 3
	if _, err := incFn.Call(); err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	r, err := getFn.Call()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if r.String() != "3" { t.Fatalf("get %s", r) }
}

func TestGoCallCloneNested(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
g := 5
mk := func() {
	n := 0
	return func() { n++; return g + n }
}
f := mk()
f()
data := [f]
`
	c1 := runScript(t, src)
	c2 := c1.Clone()
	if err := c2.Set("g", 100); err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	arr2 := c2.Get("data").Object().(*tengo.Array)
	f2 := arr2.Value[0].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	r, err := f2.Call()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if r.String() != "102" { t.Fatalf("clone %s", r) }
}

package tengo_test

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2"
	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2/stdlib"
)

func TestGoCallCompositeReturn(t *testing.T) {
	c := runScript(t, `
mk := func(x) {
	f1 := func(y) { return x + y }
	f2 := func(y) { return x * y }
	return [f1, f2]
}
`)
	fn, _ := c.Get("mk").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	ret, err := fn.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 10})
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	arr, ok := ret.(*tengo.Array)
	if !ok { t.Fatalf("type %T", ret) }
	if len(arr.Value) != 2 { t.Fatalf("len %d", len(arr.Value)) }
	f1 := arr.Value[0].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	f2 := arr.Value[1].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	r1, _ := f1.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 5})
	if r1.String() != "15" { t.Fatalf("f1 %s", r1) }
	r2, _ := f2.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 5})
	if r2.String() != "50" { t.Fatalf("f2 %s", r2) }
}

func TestGoCallNestedTransferIsolation(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
g := 5
mk := func(x) {
	n := 0
	inc := func() { n++; return g + n + x }
	return [inc, { "inc": inc }]
}
data := mk(100)
`
	c1 := runScript(t, src)
	c2 := runScript(t, `out := 1`)
	if err := c2.Set("data", c1.Get("data").Object()); err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if err := c2.Set("g", 50); err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }

	arrObj := c2.Get("data").Object()
	arr, ok := arrObj.(*tengo.Array)
	if !ok { t.Fatalf("type %T", arrObj) }
	incArr := arr.Value[0].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	r, err := incArr.Call()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if r.String() != "151" { t.Fatalf("arr inc got %s (want 50+1+100)", r) }

	m, ok := arr.Value[1].(*tengo.Map)
	if !ok { t.Fatalf("map type %T", arr.Value[1]) }
	incMap := m.Value["inc"].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	r2, err := incMap.Call()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if r2.String() != "152" { t.Fatalf("map inc got %s", r2) }
}

func TestGoCallSourceModuleExport(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
mod := import("mymod")
f := mod.add
g := 10
`
	modsrc := `
x := 1
add := func(a, b) { return a + b + x }
export { "add": add }
`
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(src))
	mods := tengo.NewModuleMap()
	mods.AddSourceModule("mymod", []byte(modsrc))
	s.SetImports(mods)
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	fn, ok := c.Get("f").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	if !ok { t.Fatalf("type %T", c.Get("f").Object()) }
	ret, err := fn.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 20}, &tengo.Int{Value: 21})
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if ret.String() != "42" { t.Fatalf("got %s", ret) }
}

func TestGoCallImportInside(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
math := import("math")
use := func(x) { return math.abs(x) }
`
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(src))
	s.SetImports(stdlib.GetModuleMap("math"))
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	fn, _ := c.Get("use").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	ret, err := fn.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: -42})
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if ret.String() != "42" { t.Fatalf("got %s", ret) }
}

func TestGoCallGlobalMutation(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
g := 5
bump := func() { g += 10; return g }
`
	c := runScript(t, src)
	fn, _ := c.Get("bump").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	r1, err := fn.Call()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if r1.String() != "15" { t.Fatalf("r1 %s", r1) }
	r2, _ := fn.Call()
	if r2.String() != "25" { t.Fatalf("r2 %s", r2) }
	if got := c.Get("g").Int(); got != 15 { t.Fatalf("g %d", got) }
}

func TestGoCallWrongArgs(t *testing.T) {
	c := runScript(t, `add := func(a, b) { return a + b }`)
	fn, _ := c.Get("add").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	_, err := fn.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 1})
	if err == nil { t.Fatal("expected error") }
	c2 := runScript(t, `v := func(a, ...rest) { return len(rest) }`)
	fn2, _ := c2.Get("v").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	_, err = fn2.Call()
	if err == nil { t.Fatal("expected error for missing required arg") }
}

func TestGoCallClosureSharingWithinInstance(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
mk := func() {
	n := 0
	inc := func() { n++; return n }
	get := func() { return n }
	return [inc, get]
}
pair := mk()
inc := pair[0]
get := pair[1]
inc()
inc()
out := get()
`
	c := runScript(t, src)
	if got := c.Get("out").Int(); got != 2 { t.Fatalf("out %d", got) }
	incFn, _ := c.Get("inc").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	getFn, _ := c.Get("get").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	if _, err := incFn.Call(); err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	r, err := getFn.Call()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if r.String() != "3" { t.Fatalf("get %s", r) }
}

cd /app && cat > zz_repro4_test.go <<'EOF'
package tengo_test

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2"
)

func TestGoCallDeepRecursion(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
fib := func(n) {
	if n < 2 { return n }
	return fib(n-1) + fib(n-2)
}
`
	c := runScript(t, src)
	fn, _ := c.Get("fib").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	ret, err := fn.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 20})
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if ret.String() != "6765" { t.Fatalf("got %s", ret) }
}

func TestGoCallMaxAllocs(t *testing.T) {
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(`
mk := func() {
	x := 0
	x++
	return x
}
`))
	s.SetMaxAllocs(0) // only 0 allocations allowed
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	fn, _ := c.Get("mk").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	_, err = fn.Call()
	if err == nil { t.Fatal("expected alloc limit error") }
	if !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "object allocation limit exceeded") {
		t.Fatalf("err: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestGoCallReturnMapWithClosures(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
mk := func(x) {
	inc := func(y) { return x + y }
	return { "inc": inc, "x": x }
}
`
	c := runScript(t, src)
	fn, _ := c.Get("mk").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	ret, err := fn.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 40})
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	m, ok := ret.(*tengo.Map)
	if !ok { t.Fatalf("type %T", ret) }
	inc := m.Value["inc"].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	r, err := inc.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 2})
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if r.String() != "42" { t.Fatalf("got %s", r) }
}

func TestGoCallRunAfter(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
g := 1
bump := func() { g += 1 }
`
	c := runScript(t, src)
	fn, _ := c.Get("bump").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	if _, err := fn.Call(); err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if got := c.Get("g").Int(); got != 2 { t.Fatalf("g=%d", got) }
	// re-run the script (resets g to 1)
	if err := c.Run(); err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if got := c.Get("g").Int(); got != 1 { t.Fatalf("after rerun g=%d", got) }
}

func TestGoCallCloneAfterGoCall(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
g := 1
f := func() { g += 1; return g }
`
	c := runScript(t, src)
	fn, _ := c.Get("f").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	if _, err := fn.Call(); err != nil { t.Fatal(err) } // g -> 2
	cl := c.Clone()
	if got := cl.Get("g").Int(); got != 2 { t.Fatalf("clone g=%d", got) }
	clFn, _ := cl.Get("f").Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	r, err := clFn.Call()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	if r.String() != "3" { t.Fatalf("got %s", r) }
	// source unchanged
	if got := c.Get("g").Int(); got != 2 { t.Fatalf("source g=%d", got) }
}

+// attachContext returns a copy of o with the given execution context attached.
+// Unlike bindContext, attachContext keeps the original free-variable pointers,
+// so closures continue to share their captured variables during VM execution.
+func (o *CompiledFunction) attachContext(ctx *functionContext) *CompiledFunction {
+	if o.ctx == ctx {
+		return o
+	}
+	return &CompiledFunction{
+		Instructions:  o.Instructions,
+		NumLocals:     o.NumLocals,
+		NumParameters: o.NumParameters,
+		VarArgs:       o.VarArgs,
+		SourceMap:     o.SourceMap,
+		Free:          o.Free,
+		ctx:           ctx,
+	}
+}
+
+// bindContext returns a copy of o bound to ctx. The captured free variables are
+// snapshotted at their current values so the copy is independent of the VM stack
+// where the closure was created. The function bytecode (instructions, constants
+// and imports) is preserved from the function's original context when one is
+// available; only globals and the allocation limit are rebound to ctx.
+func (o *CompiledFunction) bindContext(ctx *functionContext) *CompiledFunction {
+	bytecode := ctx.bytecode
+	if o.ctx != nil {
+		bytecode = o.ctx.bytecode
+	}
+	free := make([]*ObjectPtr, len(o.Free))
+	for i, p := range o.Free {
+		if p != nil {
+			val := *p.Value
+			free[i] = &ObjectPtr{Value: &val}
+		}
+	}
+	return &CompiledFunction{
+		Instructions:  append([]byte{}, o.Instructions...),
+		NumLocals:     o.NumLocals,
+		NumParameters: o.NumParameters,
+		VarArgs:       o.VarArgs,
+		SourceMap:     o.SourceMap,
+		Free:          free,
+		ctx: &functionContext{
+			bytecode:  bytecode,
+			globals:   ctx.globals,
+			maxAllocs: ctx.maxAllocs,
+		},
 	}
 }
 
@@ -626,6 +688,78 @@ func (o *CompiledFunction) CanCall() bool {
 	return true
 }
 
+// Call invokes the compiled function from Go code. The function executes with
+// the same globals, imports, closure captures, variadic behavior and error
+// formatting as it would inside the VM.
+func (o *CompiledFunction) Call(args ...Object) (Object, error) {
+	if o.ctx == nil {
+		return nil, fmt.Errorf("not callable: %s", o.TypeName())
+	}
+
+	if o.VarArgs {
+		realArgs := o.NumParameters - 1
+		if len(args) < realArgs {
+			return nil, fmt.Errorf(
+				"wrong number of arguments: want>=%d, got=%d",
+				realArgs, len(args))
+		}
+		varArgs := make([]Object, len(args)-realArgs)
+		copy(varArgs, args[realArgs:])
+		args = append(args[:realArgs], &Array{Value: varArgs})
+	} else if len(args) != o.NumParameters {
+		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
+			"wrong number of arguments: want=%d, got=%d",
+			o.NumParameters, len(args))
+	}
+
+	v := NewVM(o.ctx.bytecode, o.ctx.globals, o.ctx.maxAllocs)
+	ret, err := v.runFunction(o, args)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+
+	// bind the result (and any callable values reachable from it) to the
+	// same execution context so it remains callable from Go.
+	return bindObject(ret, o.ctx), nil
+}
+
+// bindObject recursively rebinds callable values to ctx. Composite values are
+// deep-copied so that the resulting object is isolated from the original.
+func bindObject(o Object, ctx *functionContext) Object {
+	switch o := o.(type) {
+	case *CompiledFunction:
+		return o.bindContext(ctx)
+	case *Array:
+		val := make([]Object, len(o.Value))
+		for i, e := range o.Value {
+			val[i] = bindObject(e, ctx)
+		}
+		return &Array{Value: val}
+	case *ImmutableArray:
+		val := make([]Object, len(o.Value))
+		for i, e := range o.Value {
+			val[i] = bindObject(e, ctx)
+		}
+		return &ImmutableArray{Value: val}
+	case *Map:
+		val := make(map[string]Object, len(o.Value))
+		for k, e := range o.Value {
+			val[k] = bindObject(e, ctx)
+		}
+		return &Map{Value: val}
+	case *ImmutableMap:
+		val := make(map[string]Object, len(o.Value))
+		for k, e := range o.Value {
+			val[k] = bindObject(e, ctx)
+		}
+		return &ImmutableMap{Value: val}
+	case *Error:
+		return &Error{Value: bindObject(o.Value, ctx)}
+	default:
+		return o
+	}
+}
+
 // Error represents an error value.
 type Error struct {
 	ObjectImpl

+// runFunction runs a single compiled function with the given arguments and
+// returns its return value. It is used to support invoking compiled functions
+// from Go code (CompiledFunction.Call).
+func (v *VM) runFunction(fn *CompiledFunction, args []Object) (
+	Object, error) {
+	v.stopOnReturn = true
+	v.returnValue = UndefinedValue
+	v.frames[0].fn = fn
+	v.frames[0].freeVars = fn.Free
+	v.frames[0].ip = -1
+	v.frames[0].basePointer = 0
+	if fn.ctx != nil {
+		v.frames[0].bytecode = fn.ctx.bytecode
+		v.frames[0].ctx = fn.ctx
+	} else {
+		v.frames[0].bytecode = v.bytecode
+		v.frames[0].ctx = v.ctx
+	}
+	v.curFrame = &v.frames[0]
+	v.curInsts = fn.Instructions
+	v.framesIndex = 1
+	v.ip = -1
+	v.sp = 0
+	for i, arg := range args {
+		v.stack[i] = arg
+	}
+	v.sp = fn.NumLocals
+	v.allocs = v.maxAllocs + 1
+
+	v.run()
+	atomic.StoreInt64(&v.aborting, 0)
+	if v.err != nil {
+		return nil, v.formatError(v.err)
+	}
+	ret := v.returnValue
+	if ret == nil {
+		ret = UndefinedValue
+	}
+	return ret, nil
+}
+
+// formatError wraps a runtime error with the runtime error message and the
+// source position / call stack.
+func (v *VM) formatError(err error) error {
+	filePos := v.fileSet.Position(
+		v.curFrame.fn.SourcePos(v.ip - 1))
+	err = fmt.Errorf("Runtime Error: %w\n\tat %s",
+		err, filePos)
+	for v.framesIndex > 1 {
+		v.framesIndex--
+		v.curFrame = &v.frames[v.framesIndex-1]
+		filePos = v.fileSet.Position(
+			v.curFrame.fn.SourcePos(v.curFrame.ip - 1))
+		err = fmt.Errorf("%w\n\tat %s", err, filePos)
+	}
+	return err
+}
+

STEP 94 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > compiled_function_call_test.go <<'EOF'
package tengo_test

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2"
	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2/stdlib"
)

func runCompiled(t *testing.T, src string) *tengo.Compiled {
	t.Helper()
	c, err := tengo.NewScript([]byte(src)).Run()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("run: %v", err)
	}
	return c
}

func compiledFn(t *testing.T, c *tengo.Compiled, name string) *tengo.CompiledFunction {
	t.Helper()
	fn, ok := c.Get(name).Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("variable %q is %T, not *CompiledFunction", name,
			c.Get(name).Object())
	}
	return fn
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallBasic(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `
add := func(a, b) { return a + b }
out := add(1, 2)
`)
	ret, err := compiledFn(t, c, "add").Call(
		&tengo.Int{Value: 40}, &tengo.Int{Value: 2})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := ret.String(); got != "42" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s, want 42", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallRecursion(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `
fact := func(n) {
	if n <= 1 { return 1 }
	return n * fact(n-1)
}
`)
	ret, err := compiledFn(t, c, "fact").Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 10})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := ret.String(); got != "3628800" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s, want 3628800", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallVariadic(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `
sum := func(a, ...args) {
	s := a
	for _, x in args { s += x }
	return s
}
`)
	ret, err := compiledFn(t, c, "sum").Call(
		&tengo.Int{Value: 1}, &tengo.Int{Value: 2}, &tengo.Int{Value: 3})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := ret.String(); got != "6" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s, want 6", got)
	}

	// too few arguments
	_, err = compiledFn(t, c, "sum").Call()
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected argument-count error")
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallClosure(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `
g := 5
make := func() {
	y := 10
	return func() { return g + y }
}
f := make()
`)
	ret, err := compiledFn(t, c, "f").Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := ret.String(); got != "15" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s, want 15", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallReturnedClosure(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `
make := func(x) {
	return func(y) { return x + y }
}
f := make(10)
`)
	ret, err := compiledFn(t, c, "make").Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 10})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	inner, ok := ret.(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("returned value is %T, not *CompiledFunction", ret)
	}
	r, err := inner.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 32})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := r.String(); got != "42" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s, want 42", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallReturnedComposite(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `
mk := func(x) {
	inc := func(y) { return x + y }
	mul := func(y) { return x * y }
	return [inc, { "mul": mul }]
}
`)
	ret, err := compiledFn(t, c, "mk").Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 10})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	arr, ok := ret.(*tengo.Array)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("returned value is %T, want *Array", ret)
	}
	inc := arr.Value[0].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	if r, _ := inc.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 5}); r.String() != "15" {
		t.Fatalf("inc returned %s", r)
	}
	m, ok := arr.Value[1].(*tengo.Map)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("map is %T", arr.Value[1])
	}
	mul := m.Value["mul"].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	if r, _ := mul.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 5}); r.String() != "50" {
		t.Fatalf("mul returned %s", r)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallFromGoCallback(t *testing.T) {
	apply := func(args ...tengo.Object) (tengo.Object, error) {
		fn := args[0].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
		return fn.Call(args[1])
	}
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(`
double := func(x) { return x * 2 }
gcall := import("gcall")
out := gcall.apply(double, 21)
`))
	mods := tengo.NewModuleMap()
	mods.AddBuiltinModule("gcall", map[string]tengo.Object{
		"apply": &tengo.UserFunction{Name: "apply", Value: apply},
	})
	s.SetImports(mods)
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := c.Get("out").Int(); got != 42 {
		t.Fatalf("got %d, want 42", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallSourceModuleExport(t *testing.T) {
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(`
mod := import("mymod")
f := mod.add
`))
	mods := tengo.NewModuleMap()
	mods.AddSourceModule("mymod", []byte(`
x := 1
add := func(a, b) { return a + b + x }
export { "add": add }
`))
	s.SetImports(mods)
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	ret, err := compiledFn(t, c, "f").Call(
		&tengo.Int{Value: 20}, &tengo.Int{Value: 21})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := ret.String(); got != "42" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s, want 42", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallImport(t *testing.T) {
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(`
math := import("math")
use := func(x) { return math.abs(x) }
`))
	s.SetImports(stdlib.GetModuleMap("math"))
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	ret, err := compiledFn(t, c, "use").Call(&tengo.Int{Value: -42})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := ret.String(); got != "42" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s, want 42", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallGlobalMutation(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `
g := 5
bump := func() { g += 10; return g }
`)
	fn := compiledFn(t, c, "bump")
	r1, err := fn.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r1.String() != "15" {
		t.Fatalf("first call returned %s", r1)
	}
	r2, err := fn.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r2.String() != "25" {
		t.Fatalf("second call returned %s", r2)
	}
	if got := c.Get("g").Int(); got != 25 {
		t.Fatalf("global g = %d, want 25", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallErrorFormat(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `
inner := func() { return 1 + "x" }
outer := func() { return inner() }
`)
	_, err := compiledFn(t, c, "outer").Call()
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected runtime error")
	}
	msg := err.Error()
	if !strings.Contains(msg, "Runtime Error: invalid operation: int + string") {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(msg, "at (main):2:9") || !strings.Contains(msg, "at (main):4:9") {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected stack trace: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallMaxAllocs(t *testing.T) {
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(`
mk := func() {
	x := 0
	x++
	return x
}
`))
	s.SetMaxAllocs(0)
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, err = compiledFn(t, c, "mk").Call()
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected allocation limit error")
	}
	if !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "object allocation limit exceeded") {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallCloneIsolation(t *testing.T) {
	c1 := runCompiled(t, `
g := 5
mk := func() {
	n := 0
	return func() { n++; return g + n }
}
f := mk()
f()
`)
	clone := c1.Clone()
	if err := clone.Set("g", 100); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	f1 := compiledFn(t, c1, "f")
	f2 := compiledFn(t, clone, "f")

	// source: g=5, n=1 -> 7
	r1, err := f1.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r1.String() != "7" {
		t.Fatalf("source returned %s", r1)
	}

	// clone: g=100, n snapshot=1 -> 102
	r2, err := f2.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r2.String() != "102" {
		t.Fatalf("clone returned %s", r2)
	}

	// each instance keeps its own globals and captures
	if got := c1.Get("g").Int(); got != 5 {
		t.Fatalf("source g=%d", got)
	}
	if got := clone.Get("g").Int(); got != 100 {
		t.Fatalf("clone g=%d", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallTransferIsolation(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
g := 10
mk := func() {
	n := 0
	return func() { n++; return g + n }
}
f := mk()
f()
f()
`
	c1 := runCompiled(t, src)
	fn1 := compiledFn(t, c1, "f")

	// destination compiled from the same source so global indexes match
	c2 := runCompiled(t, src)
	if err := c2.Set("f", fn1); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := c2.Set("g", 100); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	// transferred closure sees captures at transfer time (n=2) and
	// resolves globals against the destination (g=100) -> 103
	fn2 := compiledFn(t, c2, "f")
	r2, err := fn2.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r2.String() != "103" {
		t.Fatalf("destination returned %s", r2)
	}

	// source is unaffected: n=2 -> 3, g=10 -> 13
	r1, err := fn1.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r1.String() != "13" {
		t.Fatalf("source returned %s", r1)
	}
	if got := c2.Get("g").Int(); got != 100 {
		t.Fatalf("destination g=%d", got)
	}
	if got := c1.Get("g").Int(); got != 10 {
		t.Fatalf("source g=%d", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallNestedTransferIsolation(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
g := 5
mk := func(x) {
	n := 0
	inc := func() { n++; return g + n + x }
	return [inc, { "inc": inc }]
}
data := mk(100)
`
	c1 := runCompiled(t, src)
	c2 := runCompiled(t, src)
	if err := c2.Set("data", c1.Get("data").Object()); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := c2.Set("g", 50); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	arr := c2.Get("data").Object().(*tengo.Array)
	incArr := arr.Value[0].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	r, err := incArr.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r.String() != "151" {
		t.Fatalf("array closure returned %s", r)
	}

	m := arr.Value[1].(*tengo.Map)
	incMap := m.Value["inc"].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	r2, err := incMap.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// independently snapshotted capture: also starts at n=0
	if r2.String() != "151" {
		t.Fatalf("map closure returned %s", r2)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallClosureSharing(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `
mk := func() {
	n := 0
	inc := func() { n++; return n }
	get := func() { return n }
	return [inc, get]
}
pair := mk()
inc := pair[0]
get := pair[1]
inc()
inc()
out := get()
`)
	if got := c.Get("out").Int(); got != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("in-script shared capture out=%d", got)
	}
	incFn := compiledFn(t, c, "inc")
	getFn := compiledFn(t, c, "get")
	if _, err := incFn.Call(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	r, err := getFn.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// Go-side calls still share the same capture
	if r.String() != "3" {
		t.Fatalf("shared capture returned %s", r)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallWrongArgs(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `add := func(a, b) { return a + b }`)
	_, err := compiledFn(t, c, "add").Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 1})
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected error for wrong number of arguments")
	}
}

package tengo_test

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2"
)

func TestEdgeNestedClosureCapture(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
mk := func() {
	n := 0
	inc := func() { n++; return n }
	outer := func() { return inc() }
	return outer
}
f := mk()
f()
`
	c1 := runCompiled(t, src)
	f1 := compiledFn(t, c1, "f")
	c2 := runCompiled(t, src)
	if err := c2.Set("f", f1); err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	f2 := compiledFn(t, c2, "f")

	// after f() once, n=1. transferring should snapshot n=1.
	// calling f2 should make n=2 and return 2; calling f1 should make its n=2 and return 2.
	r2, err := f2.Call()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	t.Log("f2:", r2.String())
	r1, err := f1.Call()
	if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
	t.Log("f1:", r1.String())
}
EOF

old='''// bindContext returns a copy of o bound to ctx. The captured free variables are
// snapshotted at their current values so the copy is independent of the VM stack
// where the closure was created. The function bytecode (instructions, constants
// and imports) is preserved from the function's original context when one is
// available; only globals and the allocation limit are rebound to ctx.
func (o *CompiledFunction) bindContext(ctx *functionContext) *CompiledFunction {
	bytecode := ctx.bytecode
	if o.ctx != nil {
		bytecode = o.ctx.bytecode
	}
	free := make([]*ObjectPtr, len(o.Free))
	for i, p := range o.Free {
		if p != nil && p.Value != nil {
			val := *p.Value
			free[i] = &ObjectPtr{Value: &val}
		}
	}
	return &CompiledFunction{
		Instructions:  append([]byte{}, o.Instructions...),
		NumLocals:     o.NumLocals,
		NumParameters: o.NumParameters,
		VarArgs:       o.VarArgs,
		SourceMap:     o.SourceMap,
		Free:          free,
		ctx: &functionContext{
			bytecode:  bytecode,
			globals:   ctx.globals,
			maxAllocs: ctx.maxAllocs,
		},
	}
}'''
new='''// bindContext returns a copy of o bound to ctx. The captured free variables are
// snapshotted at their current values (recursively rebinding any callable
// values) so the copy is independent of the VM stack where the closure was
// created. The function bytecode (instructions, constants and imports) is
// preserved from the function's original context when one is available; only
// globals and the allocation limit are rebound to ctx.
func (o *CompiledFunction) bindContext(ctx *functionContext) *CompiledFunction {
	return o.bindContextRec(ctx, make(map[Object]Object))
}

func (o *CompiledFunction) bindContextRec(
	ctx *functionContext,
	seen map[Object]Object,
) *CompiledFunction {
	if bound, ok := seen[o]; ok {
		if b, ok := bound.(*CompiledFunction); ok {
			return b
		}
	}
	bytecode := ctx.bytecode
	if o.ctx != nil {
		bytecode = o.ctx.bytecode
	}
	bound := &CompiledFunction{
		Instructions:  append([]byte{}, o.Instructions...),
		NumLocals:     o.NumLocals,
		NumParameters: o.NumParameters,
		VarArgs:       o.VarArgs,
		SourceMap:     o.SourceMap,
		ctx: &functionContext{
			bytecode:  bytecode,
			globals:   ctx.globals,
			maxAllocs: ctx.maxAllocs,
		},
	}
	seen[o] = bound
	free := make([]*ObjectPtr, len(o.Free))
	for i, p := range o.Free {
		if p != nil && p.Value != nil {
			val := bindObjectRec(*p.Value, ctx, seen)
			free[i] = &ObjectPtr{Value: &val}
		}
	}
	bound.Free = free
	return bound
}'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)

old='''// bindObject recursively rebinds callable values to ctx. Composite values are
// deep-copied so that the resulting object is isolated from the original.
func bindObject(o Object, ctx *functionContext) Object {
	if o == nil {
		return nil
	}
	switch o := o.(type) {
	case *CompiledFunction:
		return o.bindContext(ctx)
	case *Array:
		val := make([]Object, len(o.Value))
		for i, e := range o.Value {
			val[i] = bindObject(e, ctx)
		}
		return &Array{Value: val}
	case *ImmutableArray:
		val := make([]Object, len(o.Value))
		for i, e := range o.Value {
			val[i] = bindObject(e, ctx)
		}
		return &ImmutableArray{Value: val}
	case *Map:
		val := make(map[string]Object, len(o.Value))
		for k, e := range o.Value {
			val[k] = bindObject(e, ctx)
		}
		return &Map{Value: val}
	case *ImmutableMap:
		val := make(map[string]Object, len(o.Value))
		for k, e := range o.Value {
			val[k] = bindObject(e, ctx)
		}
		return &ImmutableMap{Value: val}
	case *Error:
		val := bindObject(o.Value, ctx)
		return &Error{Value: val}
	default:
		return o
	}
}'''
new='''// bindObject recursively rebinds callable values to ctx. Composite values are
// deep-copied so that the resulting object is isolated from the original.
func bindObject(o Object, ctx *functionContext) Object {
	return bindObjectRec(o, ctx, make(map[Object]Object))
}

func bindObjectRec(
	o Object,
	ctx *functionContext,
	seen map[Object]Object,
) Object {
	if o == nil {
		return nil
	}
	switch o := o.(type) {
	case *CompiledFunction:
		return o.bindContextRec(ctx, seen)
	case *Array:
		if bound, ok := seen[o]; ok {
			return bound
		}
		arr := &Array{Value: make([]Object, len(o.Value))}
		seen[o] = arr
		for i, e := range o.Value {
			arr.Value[i] = bindObjectRec(e, ctx, seen)
		}
		return arr
	case *ImmutableArray:
		if bound, ok := seen[o]; ok {
			return bound
		}
		arr := &ImmutableArray{Value: make([]Object, len(o.Value))}
		seen[o] = arr
		for i, e := range o.Value {
			arr.Value[i] = bindObjectRec(e, ctx, seen)
		}
		return arr
	case *Map:
		if bound, ok := seen[o]; ok {
			return bound
		}
		m := &Map{Value: make(map[string]Object, len(o.Value))}
		seen[o] = m
		for k, e := range o.Value {
			m.Value[k] = bindObjectRec(e, ctx, seen)
		}
		return m
	case *ImmutableMap:
		if bound, ok := seen[o]; ok {
			return bound
		}
		m := &ImmutableMap{Value: make(map[string]Object, len(o.Value))}
		seen[o] = m
		for k, e := range o.Value {
			m.Value[k] = bindObjectRec(e, ctx, seen)
		}
		return m
	case *Error:
		val := bindObjectRec(o.Value, ctx, seen)
		return &Error{Value: val}
	default:
		return o
	}
}'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)

open(p,'w').write(s)
print("done")

cd /app && cat >> compiled_function_call_test.go <<'EOF'

func TestCompiledFunctionCallNestedClosureIsolation(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
mk := func() {
	n := 0
	inc := func() { n++; return n }
	outer := func() { return inc() }
	return outer
}
f := mk()
f()
`
	c1 := runCompiled(t, src)
	f1 := compiledFn(t, c1, "f")

	c2 := runCompiled(t, src)
	if err := c2.Set("f", f1); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	f2 := compiledFn(t, c2, "f")

	// both instances start with n=1 (snapshotted for the destination) and
	// each increment their own copy
	r2, err := f2.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r2.String() != "2" {
		t.Fatalf("destination returned %s", r2)
	}
	r1, err := f1.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r1.String() != "2" {
		t.Fatalf("source returned %s", r1)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallRecursiveClosureTransfer(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
mk := func() {
	f := func(n) {
		if n <= 1 { return 1 }
		return n * f(n-1)
	}
	return f
}
f := mk()
`
	c1 := runCompiled(t, src)
	f1 := compiledFn(t, c1, "f")
	c2 := runCompiled(t, src)
	if err := c2.Set("f", f1); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	f2 := compiledFn(t, c2, "f")
	ret, err := f2.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 5})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if ret.String() != "120" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s, want 120", ret)
	}
}
EOF
gofmt -w compiled_function_call_test.go && rm -f zz_edge_test.go zz_edge2_test.go && go test ./... 2>&1 | tail -12

func TestCompiledFunctionCallAddTransfer(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
g := 10
mk := func() {
	n := 0
	return func() { n++; return g + n }
}
f := mk()
f()
`
	c1 := runCompiled(t, src)
	fn1 := compiledFn(t, c1, "f")

	// Add a callable value to a fresh script. The function is rebound to the
	// new instance, so it resolves globals against the new instance.
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(`
out := f()
`))
	if err := s.Add("f", fn1); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	c2, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	fn2 := compiledFn(t, c2, "f")
	ret, err := fn2.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if ret.String() != "11" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s, want 11", ret)
	}
}
EOF

package tengo_test

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2"
	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2/stdlib"
)

func runCompiled(t *testing.T, src string) *tengo.Compiled {
	t.Helper()
	c, err := tengo.NewScript([]byte(src)).Run()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("run: %v", err)
	}
	return c
}

func compiledFn(
	t *testing.T,
	c *tengo.Compiled,
	name string,
) *tengo.CompiledFunction {
	t.Helper()
	fn, ok := c.Get(name).Object().(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("variable %q is %T, not *CompiledFunction", name,
			c.Get(name).Object())
	}
	return fn
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallBasic(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `
add := func(a, b) { return a + b }
out := add(1, 2)
`)
	ret, err := compiledFn(t, c, "add").Call(
		&tengo.Int{Value: 40}, &tengo.Int{Value: 2})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := ret.String(); got != "42" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s, want 42", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallRecursion(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `
fact := func(n) {
	if n <= 1 { return 1 }
	return n * fact(n-1)
}
`)
	ret, err := compiledFn(t, c, "fact").Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 10})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := ret.String(); got != "3628800" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s, want 3628800", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallVariadic(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `
sum := func(a, ...args) {
	s := a
	for _, x in args { s += x }
	return s
}
`)
	ret, err := compiledFn(t, c, "sum").Call(
		&tengo.Int{Value: 1}, &tengo.Int{Value: 2}, &tengo.Int{Value: 3})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := ret.String(); got != "6" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s, want 6", got)
	}

	// too few arguments
	_, err = compiledFn(t, c, "sum").Call()
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected argument-count error")
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallClosure(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `
g := 5
make := func() {
	y := 10
	return func() { return g + y }
}
f := make()
`)
	ret, err := compiledFn(t, c, "f").Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := ret.String(); got != "15" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s, want 15", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallReturnedClosure(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `
make := func(x) {
	return func(y) { return x + y }
}
f := make(10)
`)
	ret, err := compiledFn(t, c, "make").Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 10})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	inner, ok := ret.(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("returned value is %T, not *CompiledFunction", ret)
	}
	r, err := inner.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 32})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := r.String(); got != "42" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s, want 42", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallReturnedComposite(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `
mk := func(x) {
	inc := func(y) { return x + y }
	mul := func(y) { return x * y }
	return [inc, { "mul": mul }]
}
`)
	ret, err := compiledFn(t, c, "mk").Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 10})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	arr, ok := ret.(*tengo.Array)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("returned value is %T, want *Array", ret)
	}
	inc := arr.Value[0].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	if r, _ := inc.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 5}); r.String() != "15" {
		t.Fatalf("inc returned %s", r)
	}
	m, ok := arr.Value[1].(*tengo.Map)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("map is %T", arr.Value[1])
	}
	mul := m.Value["mul"].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	if r, _ := mul.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 5}); r.String() != "50" {
		t.Fatalf("mul returned %s", r)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallFromGoCallback(t *testing.T) {
	apply := func(args ...tengo.Object) (tengo.Object, error) {
		fn := args[0].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
		return fn.Call(args[1])
	}
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(`
double := func(x) { return x * 2 }
gcall := import("gcall")
out := gcall.apply(double, 21)
`))
	mods := tengo.NewModuleMap()
	mods.AddBuiltinModule("gcall", map[string]tengo.Object{
		"apply": &tengo.UserFunction{Name: "apply", Value: apply},
	})
	s.SetImports(mods)
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := c.Get("out").Int(); got != 42 {
		t.Fatalf("got %d, want 42", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallSourceModuleExport(t *testing.T) {
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(`
mod := import("mymod")
f := mod.add
`))
	mods := tengo.NewModuleMap()
	mods.AddSourceModule("mymod", []byte(`
x := 1
add := func(a, b) { return a + b + x }
export { "add": add }
`))
	s.SetImports(mods)
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	ret, err := compiledFn(t, c, "f").Call(
		&tengo.Int{Value: 20}, &tengo.Int{Value: 21})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := ret.String(); got != "42" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s, want 42", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallImport(t *testing.T) {
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(`
math := import("math")
use := func(x) { return math.abs(x) }
`))
	s.SetImports(stdlib.GetModuleMap("math"))
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	ret, err := compiledFn(t, c, "use").Call(&tengo.Int{Value: -42})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := ret.String(); got != "42" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s, want 42", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallGlobalMutation(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `
g := 5
bump := func() { g += 10; return g }
`)
	fn := compiledFn(t, c, "bump")
	r1, err := fn.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r1.String() != "15" {
		t.Fatalf("first call returned %s", r1)
	}
	r2, err := fn.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r2.String() != "25" {
		t.Fatalf("second call returned %s", r2)
	}
	if got := c.Get("g").Int(); got != 25 {
		t.Fatalf("global g = %d, want 25", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallErrorFormat(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `
inner := func() { return 1 + "x" }
outer := func() { return inner() }
`)
	_, err := compiledFn(t, c, "outer").Call()
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected runtime error")
	}
	msg := err.Error()
	if !strings.Contains(msg, "Runtime Error: invalid operation: int + string") {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
	}
	if strings.Count(msg, "at (main):") != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected stack trace: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallMaxAllocs(t *testing.T) {
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(`
mk := func() {
	x := 0
	x++
	return x
}
`))
	s.SetMaxAllocs(0)
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, err = compiledFn(t, c, "mk").Call()
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected allocation limit error")
	}
	if !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "object allocation limit exceeded") {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallCloneIsolation(t *testing.T) {
	c1 := runCompiled(t, `
g := 5
mk := func() {
	n := 0
	return func() { n++; return g + n }
}
f := mk()
f()
`)
	clone := c1.Clone()
	if err := clone.Set("g", 100); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	f1 := compiledFn(t, c1, "f")
	f2 := compiledFn(t, clone, "f")

	// source: g=5, n=1 -> 7
	r1, err := f1.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r1.String() != "7" {
		t.Fatalf("source returned %s", r1)
	}

	// clone: g=100, n snapshot=1 -> 102
	r2, err := f2.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r2.String() != "102" {
		t.Fatalf("clone returned %s", r2)
	}

	// each instance keeps its own globals and captures
	if got := c1.Get("g").Int(); got != 5 {
		t.Fatalf("source g=%d", got)
	}
	if got := clone.Get("g").Int(); got != 100 {
		t.Fatalf("clone g=%d", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallTransferIsolation(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
g := 10
mk := func() {
	n := 0
	return func() { n++; return g + n }
}
f := mk()
f()
f()
`
	c1 := runCompiled(t, src)
	fn1 := compiledFn(t, c1, "f")

	// destination compiled from the same source so global indexes match
	c2 := runCompiled(t, src)
	if err := c2.Set("f", fn1); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := c2.Set("g", 100); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	// transferred closure sees captures at transfer time (n=2) and
	// resolves globals against the destination (g=100) -> 103
	fn2 := compiledFn(t, c2, "f")
	r2, err := fn2.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r2.String() != "103" {
		t.Fatalf("destination returned %s", r2)
	}

	// source is unaffected: n=2 -> 3, g=10 -> 13
	r1, err := fn1.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r1.String() != "13" {
		t.Fatalf("source returned %s", r1)
	}
	if got := c2.Get("g").Int(); got != 100 {
		t.Fatalf("destination g=%d", got)
	}
	if got := c1.Get("g").Int(); got != 10 {
		t.Fatalf("source g=%d", got)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallNestedTransferIsolation(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
g := 5
mk := func(x) {
	n := 0
	inc := func() { n++; return g + n + x }
	return [inc, { "inc": inc }]
}
data := mk(100)
`
	c1 := runCompiled(t, src)
	c2 := runCompiled(t, src)
	if err := c2.Set("data", c1.Get("data").Object()); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := c2.Set("g", 50); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	arr := c2.Get("data").Object().(*tengo.Array)
	incArr := arr.Value[0].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	r, err := incArr.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r.String() != "151" {
		t.Fatalf("array closure returned %s", r)
	}

	m := arr.Value[1].(*tengo.Map)
	incMap := m.Value["inc"].(*tengo.CompiledFunction)
	r2, err := incMap.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// both references resolve to the same rebound closure, so the capture
	// is shared (n was already 1)
	if r2.String() != "152" {
		t.Fatalf("map closure returned %s", r2)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallClosureSharing(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `
mk := func() {
	n := 0
	inc := func() { n++; return n }
	get := func() { return n }
	return [inc, get]
}
pair := mk()
inc := pair[0]
get := pair[1]
inc()
inc()
out := get()
`)
	if got := c.Get("out").Int(); got != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("in-script shared capture out=%d", got)
	}
	incFn := compiledFn(t, c, "inc")
	getFn := compiledFn(t, c, "get")
	if _, err := incFn.Call(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	r, err := getFn.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// Go-side calls still share the same capture
	if r.String() != "3" {
		t.Fatalf("shared capture returned %s", r)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallWrongArgs(t *testing.T) {
	c := runCompiled(t, `add := func(a, b) { return a + b }`)
	_, err := compiledFn(t, c, "add").Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 1})
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected error for wrong number of arguments")
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallAddTransfer(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
mk := func() {
	n := 0
	return func() { n++; return n }
}
f := mk()
f()
`
	c1 := runCompiled(t, src)
	fn1 := compiledFn(t, c1, "f")

	// Add a callable value to a fresh script. The closure is rebound to the
	// new instance and its captured free variable is snapshotted at the
	// current value (1).
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(`
out := f()
`))
	if err := s.Add("f", fn1); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	c2, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	fn2 := compiledFn(t, c2, "f")
	ret, err := fn2.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if ret.String() != "2" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s, want 2", ret)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallNestedClosureIsolation(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
mk := func() {
	n := 0
	inc := func() { n++; return n }
	outer := func() { return inc() }
	return outer
}
f := mk()
f()
`
	c1 := runCompiled(t, src)
	f1 := compiledFn(t, c1, "f")

	c2 := runCompiled(t, src)
	if err := c2.Set("f", f1); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	f2 := compiledFn(t, c2, "f")

	// both instances start with n=1 (snapshotted for the destination) and
	// each increment their own copy
	r2, err := f2.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r2.String() != "2" {
		t.Fatalf("destination returned %s", r2)
	}
	r1, err := f1.Call()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r1.String() != "2" {
		t.Fatalf("source returned %s", r1)
	}
}

func TestCompiledFunctionCallRecursiveClosureTransfer(t *testing.T) {
	src := `
mk := func() {
	f := func(n) {
		if n <= 1 { return 1 }
		return n * f(n-1)
	}
	return f
}
f := mk()
`
	c1 := runCompiled(t, src)
	f1 := compiledFn(t, c1, "f")
	c2 := runCompiled(t, src)
	if err := c2.Set("f", f1); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	f2 := compiledFn(t, c2, "f")
	ret, err := f2.Call(&tengo.Int{Value: 5})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if ret.String() != "120" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s, want 120", ret)
	}
}

// functionContext stores the execution context (bytecode, globals and
// allocation limit) needed to invoke a CompiledFunction from Go code outside
// of a running VM.
type functionContext struct {
	bytecode  *Bytecode
	globals   []Object
	maxAllocs int64
}

// CompiledFunction represents a compiled function.
type CompiledFunction struct {
	ObjectImpl
	Instructions  []byte
	NumLocals     int // number of local variables (including function parameters)
	NumParameters int
	VarArgs       bool
	SourceMap     map[int]parser.Pos
	Free          []*ObjectPtr
	ctx           *functionContext
}

// TypeName returns the name of the type.
func (o *CompiledFunction) TypeName() string {
	return "compiled-function"
}

func (o *CompiledFunction) String() string {
	return "<compiled-function>"
}

// Size of the compiled function in bytes
// (as much as we can calculate it without reflection and black magic)
func (o *CompiledFunction) Size() int64 {
	return int64(len(o.Instructions) + len(o.SourceMap) + len(o.Free))
}

// Copy returns a copy of the type.
func (o *CompiledFunction) Copy() Object {
	return &CompiledFunction{
		Instructions:  append([]byte{}, o.Instructions...),
		NumLocals:     o.NumLocals,
		NumParameters: o.NumParameters,
		VarArgs:       o.VarArgs,
		SourceMap:     o.SourceMap,
		Free:          append([]*ObjectPtr{}, o.Free...), // DO NOT Copy() of elements; these are variable pointers
		ctx:           o.ctx,
	}
}

// attachContext returns a copy of o with the given execution context attached.
// Unlike bindContext, attachContext keeps the original free-variable pointers,
// so closures continue to share their captured variables during VM execution.
func (o *CompiledFunction) attachContext(ctx *functionContext) *CompiledFunction {
	if o.ctx == ctx {
		return o
	}
	return &CompiledFunction{
		Instructions:  o.Instructions,
		NumLocals:     o.NumLocals,
		NumParameters: o.NumParameters,
		VarArgs:       o.VarArgs,
		SourceMap:     o.SourceMap,
		Free:          o.Free,
		ctx:           ctx,
	}
}

// bindContext returns a copy of o bound to ctx. The captured free variables are
// snapshotted at their current values (recursively rebinding any callable
// values) so the copy is independent of the VM stack where the closure was
// created. The function bytecode (instructions, constants and imports) is
// preserved from the function's original context when one is available; only
// globals and the allocation limit are rebound to ctx.
func (o *CompiledFunction) bindContext(ctx *functionContext) *CompiledFunction {
	return o.bindContextRec(ctx, make(map[Object]Object))
}

func (o *CompiledFunction) bindContextRec(
	ctx *functionContext,
	seen map[Object]Object,
) *CompiledFunction {
	if bound, ok := seen[o]; ok {
		if b, ok := bound.(*CompiledFunction); ok {
			return b
		}
	}
	bytecode := ctx.bytecode
	if o.ctx != nil {
		bytecode = o.ctx.bytecode
	}
	bound := &CompiledFunction{
		Instructions:  append([]byte{}, o.Instructions...),
		NumLocals:     o.NumLocals,
		NumParameters: o.NumParameters,
		VarArgs:       o.VarArgs,
		SourceMap:     o.SourceMap,
		ctx: &functionContext{
			bytecode:  bytecode,
			globals:   ctx.globals,
			maxAllocs: ctx.maxAllocs,
		},
	}
	seen[o] = bound
	free := make([]*ObjectPtr, len(o.Free))
	for i, p := range o.Free {
		if p != nil && p.Value != nil {
			val := bindObjectRec(*p.Value, ctx, seen)
			free[i] = &ObjectPtr{Value: &val}
		}
	}
	bound.Free = free
	return bound
}

// Equals returns true if the value of the type is equal to the value of
// another object.
func (o *CompiledFunction) Equals(_ Object) bool {
	return false
}

// SourcePos returns the source position of the instruction at ip.
func (o *CompiledFunction) SourcePos(ip int) parser.Pos {
	for ip >= 0 {
		if p, ok := o.SourceMap[ip]; ok {
			return p
		}
		ip--
	}
	return parser.NoPos
}

// CanCall returns whether the Object can be Called.
func (o *CompiledFunction) CanCall() bool {
	return true
}

// Call invokes the compiled function from Go code. The function executes with
// the same globals, imports, closure captures, variadic behavior and error
// formatting as it would inside the VM.
func (o *CompiledFunction) Call(args ...Object) (Object, error) {
	if o.ctx == nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("not callable: %s", o.TypeName())
	}

	if o.VarArgs {
		realArgs := o.NumParameters - 1
		if len(args) < realArgs {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf(
				"wrong number of arguments: want>=%d, got=%d",
				realArgs, len(args))
		}
		varArgs := make([]Object, len(args)-realArgs)
		copy(varArgs, args[realArgs:])
		args = append(args[:realArgs], &Array{Value: varArgs})
	} else if len(args) != o.NumParameters {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
			"wrong number of arguments: want=%d, got=%d",
			o.NumParameters, len(args))
	}

	v := NewVM(o.ctx.bytecode, o.ctx.globals, o.ctx.maxAllocs)
	ret, err := v.runFunction(o, args)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	// bind the result (and any callable values reachable from it) to the
	// same execution context so it remains callable from Go.
	return bindObject(ret, o.ctx), nil
}

// bindObject recursively rebinds callable values to ctx. Composite values are
// deep-copied so that the resulting object is isolated from the original.
func bindObject(o Object, ctx *functionContext) Object {
	return bindObjectRec(o, ctx, make(map[Object]Object))
}

func bindObjectRec(
	o Object,
	ctx *functionContext,
	seen map[Object]Object,
) Object {
	if o == nil {
		return nil
	}
	switch o := o.(type) {
	case *CompiledFunction:
		return o.bindContextRec(ctx, seen)
	case *Array:
		if bound, ok := seen[o]; ok {
			return bound
		}
		arr := &Array{Value: make([]Object, len(o.Value))}
		seen[o] = arr
		for i, e := range o.Value {
			arr.Value[i] = bindObjectRec(e, ctx, seen)
		}
		return arr
	case *ImmutableArray:
		if bound, ok := seen[o]; ok {
			return bound
		}
		arr := &ImmutableArray{Value: make([]Object, len(o.Value))}
		seen[o] = arr
		for i, e := range o.Value {
			arr.Value[i] = bindObjectRec(e, ctx, seen)
		}
		return arr
	case *Map:
		if bound, ok := seen[o]; ok {
			return bound
		}
		m := &Map{Value: make(map[string]Object, len(o.Value))}
		seen[o] = m
		for k, e := range o.Value {
			m.Value[k] = bindObjectRec(e, ctx, seen)
		}
		return m
	case *ImmutableMap:
		if bound, ok := seen[o]; ok {
			return bound

END STEP
